> @krystian9924 I think the bigger thing to understand is that promoting the concept that "we are free because we are allowed to discuss things" is part of the playbook for the parasite/predator class. It's an integral part of the game they are playing. Sure, for many people it can cause learned helplessness, but I generally tend to look at it like this:  
>   
> If I'm in a town that's controlled by the mob, a situation where most people aren't aware of this fact, and one night I witness one of the mob members killing someone and/or dumping the body. What's going to happen if I go to the police or start telling people about it? Likely nothing will happen to me (maybe I'll get ignored, but there is a possibility I could be killed), but if nothing comes of it despite holding a belief that the town's institutions function a certain way, and no investigation or arrests happen,  I will become internally confused because I don't know the actually-existing rules of the game/system. I continue believing in one set of rules, while another set of rules are actually in play.  
>   
> I personally would rather have a better understanding of the rules that are in play, even if there really isn't much I can do about that specifically at this moment in time. At least my energies can be focused on what I do have some REAL and direct control over.  
>   
> Is it a net positive for evil in that situation? I can't provide a definitive answer to that, but for me I'd rather strive to live more aligned with truth than fantasy.

Adding on to what the Professor said, there's so many 33s surrounding the nuke, once you start noticing:
- Trinity test site was at 33° N Longitude
- Trinity test date was on July 16th 1945 = 7+1+6+1+9+4+5 = 33
- Trinity test time was at 5:29am local time - the 329/330th minute of the day
- First nuke was dropped at Hiroshima which is also at ~33° N Longitude
- First nuke was dropped on Aug 6th 1945 = 8+6+1+9+4+5 = 33
- Second nuke was dropped at Nagosaki, also at 33° N Longitude

That's 6 times 33 was used in relation to key dates, times and locations around the first nuclear bombs created. At what point does it move past coincidence? 🤔

### **@danilog590** | 👍 6 likes

Why the astronauts couldn’t see the stars from the moon. -  a person standing on the sunlit (bright) side of the Moon during lunar daytime would generally not be able to see the stars with the naked eye. The lunar sky is pitch black—there’s no atmosphere to scatter sunlight into a blue daytime glow like on Earth—but the surface itself is intensely bright, reflecting sunlight with a luminance of about 2,500–5,000 nits (similar to fresh snow under full sun). This overwhelming brightness causes the human pupil to constrict sharply, adapting the eye to high light levels and reducing sensitivity to faint objects like stars.

### **@Lexalis_Belalt** | 👍 69 likes
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ihh1fdW4-cA&lc=UgyeGyigD12Kei2bDD54AaABAg)

About the moon landing, there are actually quite simple explaination about the arguments that prof Jiang presents us there.

1) The missing stars
If we can’t see any stars in the footage, it’s simply because it happened during the lunar day!
Just like on Earth, there’s a day/night cycle on the Moon — only it lasts about 3 weeks instead of 24 hours.
As Buzz Aldrin later explained, he couldn’t see the stars without optical instruments because he was blinded by the sunlight reflecting off the Earth and the lunar surface.
And if he looks stressed in front of the journalists, keep in mind these guys had just risked their lives in space and weren’t exactly used to that kind of attention.

The “too fast” acceleration
That can be explained mainly by two points:

The Moon’s gravity is only 1/6 of Earth’s.

There’s no atmosphere, so no air resistance to slow down the ascent stage, which was actually quite light — about 4.7 tons, roughly the weight of an adult African elephant.
That’s also why the astronauts move and jump in that weird “slow and floaty” way.

The Moon landing is an extremely well-documented event, and we have hundreds of kilos of lunar samples.
I’ve personally seen one displayed at the CEA (a French space research lab), and I can assure you that French scientists would have loved to expose such a ridiculous lie from NASA or the CIA if it had really been one.

So please… be wary.
We should all be cautious before beleiving or posting anything onto the internet even comin from such a well educated and remarkable person as Prof Jiang.

I think personnaly that the last two video on this channel, this one and "The Theory of Everything" have huge logical flaws and that these poor students are kinda being taken adventage of.

At this point, I almost expect a video starting like:

“Hi guys, remember my last two courses? Well, it was all a trick, I brainwashed you.”
“Wait… seriously? And what about the rest of your courses?”
“Well, maybe, maybe not. Maybe you should go read Dante and Plato yourselves — I could have tricked you there too. :)”

Triangular numbers are: 1,3,6,10,15,21,28,36,45,66,78 etc…

You get a triangular number by adding all the dots arranged in an equilateral triangle. With each subsequent row representing the nth number.

Thus 9, 11, 22, and 33 (all numbers relevant to the events described) are not triangular numbers. 

The astronauts did not see any stars because it was during lunar day. The glare from the sun constricts the pupil, allowing limited light passage. 

The ascension of the module leaving lunar surface without much resistance is because the gravity on the Moon is very weak compared to Earth.

The sky on the moon looks black although it is daylight because the moon’s atmosphere is pretty much non-existent, so it does not scatter solar rays to the degree Earth does.

There were 6 more manned lunar missions since Apollo 11, one of which never landed because a fire broke out.

To survive the Van Allen belt these spacecrafts travel at an exorbitant speed to limit the time of exposure to radiation. Also, the amour of the spacecrafts are equipped with protective material to withstand radiation.

The Twin Towers pancaked due to the weight of the planes.

Why would the U.S government risk economic disaster by targeting the World Trade Center? Why not target the biggest football stadiums in America or Hollywood to rally the American citizens for war as these are the beloved symbols of American culture.

There were in fact many cradles of civilization all existed around the same time as Mesopotamia:
-The Nile river in Ancient Egypt
-The Indus Valley in Ancient India
-The Yangzte River in Ancient China
-Caral Supe in now South America
-Omec in North America

### **@Matthew-YT79** | 👍 1 likes
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ihh1fdW4-cA&lc=UgzYYGU0V2jfVJXQ-Hh4AaABAg)

Thank you for this lecture series professor Jiang. I have only discovered it today but I have learned a lot already and I know I will learn much more from watching the entire series.

I do have to point out a mistake though, with regard to your information on 9/11. The steel from the Twin Towers was not shipped to China. This is disinformation put out by the "9/11 truth movement". The buildings were dustified i.e. turned to dust using directed energy technology. This is not a theory or an opinion but it is proven by various kinds of empirical data, which does include the video footage of what we have been told, repeatedly, was a controlled demolition. But it was not a controlled demolition.

There are photos taken on 9/11 before 5:20pm (because WTC7 can be seen in the background still standing) and the debris field of the 110-storey towers is 1 or 2 storeys high. If it was a controlled demolition you would expect a pile about 11-13 storeys high. There is also seismic data which proves that there was no controlled demolition because the seismic signal shows that the 110-storey buildings did not slam to the ground.

There is much more information in Dr. Judy Wood's book Where Did The Towers Go? Evidence of Directed Free-Energy Technology on 9/11, which has its own website and is also on archive.org in PDF format. Dr. Wood examined all of the evidence and carried out a forensic analysis of the data and published this book in 2010. There are a lot of holes in these theories and there are many more nuances to each scenario than he notes here. Supposition like "he didn't look like a killer" or "why do they seem so nervous?" are not facts but loose projection of emotions which are not based in fact. I am not saying Prof. Jiang doesn't have some interesting theories and food for thought but they are theories and ironically indicative of some of the reasons conspiracy theories start.

For example, re 9/11 "the NY Port Authority had been removing the remaining asbestos floor-by-floor since the 1980s whenever a tenant moved out or a floor was renovated. It wasn't a single, massive emergency project; it was a standard maintenance procedure for older New York skyscrapers. Silverstein paid $3.2 billion for the lease. If the buildings were truly a liability that cost more than their value to fix, he would not have been able to secure the massive loans from lenders like GMAC to fund the deal."

As per the third building, "the internal structure collapsed first because the third building was hit by debris from the fires raging in the other two (which you can see in videos where the "penthouse" on top drops a few seconds before the rest of the building). By the time the exterior walls fell, they were falling into a hollow space, which created the "straight down" appearance."

There is no doubt there is corruption at the highest levels in the most evil forms but facts still matter - more than ever  - and
counter facts can be found simply by searching for them. Do your own research, folks.

> "Mirrors on the moon" refer to the Laser Ranging Retroreflectors (LRRR) left by Apollo 11, 14, 15, and Soviet Lunokhod missions, which are special arrays of corner-cube prisms that reflect laser beams directly back to their source on Earth, allowing scientists to precisely measure the Earth-Moon distance and study lunar dynamics, proving the moon is slowly drifting away. These reflectors are crucial scientific instruments, still in use today, that function without power and provide ongoing data on the Moon's orbit, Earth's rotation, and tests of general relativity, according to NASA

### **@AleksandarJovic_BorUtd**
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ihh1fdW4-cA&lc=UgwoIkNn5ehNHtxpoL54AaABAg)

One of the most common myths about flights to the Moon claims that the Van Allen radiation belts are a deadly barrier that humans cannot pass through alive. That is not true.
Earth’s magnetic field does indeed trap charged particles and create radiation belts around the planet, but the danger does not depend only on their existence. It also depends on how long you spend passing through them, the trajectory you take, and how much shielding the spacecraft provides.
On the way to the Moon, a spacecraft does not spend much time in the most exposed region. At a speed of about 11 kilometers per second, passing through the core of the belt takes about eight minutes, and crossing the entire region takes roughly an hour.
In addition, the trajectory is planned to avoid areas with the highest radiation levels. So, spacecraft do not pass randomly through the worst possible part, but instead choose a safer path in advance.
Interestingly, Artemis I also provided concrete data on this. The spacecraft carried mannequins (and yes, for conspiracy theorists, “mannequin” is the correct term in this context, and I am not an AI agent) named Helga and Zohar, equipped with active dosimeters, instruments that measure radiation exposure.
Preliminary data showed that the spacecraft’s shielding and the short transit time were sufficient to keep the radiation dose within safe limits. A very illustrative comparison is often used: if you’ve ever had a CT scan, you received roughly a similar dose of radiation as during passage through the Van Allen belts.
That is precisely why these belts are not proof that missions to the Moon are impossible, but rather another challenge solved through physics, calculation, and engineering.

### **@freudenbotschaft1086**
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ihh1fdW4-cA&lc=UgxTpI_0OieYk3Xu8jF4AaABAg)

Is it correct that the following locations all lie on the 33rd parallel north, and could you, in any case (whether it's true or not), list all these places for me with their actual exact position north of the Equator? a) Testing ground for the first atomic bomb detonation, b) Dallas, c) Jerusalem, d) Damascus, e) Baghdad, f) Confluence of the Euphrates and Tigris, g) Hiroshima and Nagasaki



________________________________________

Gemini (2.5 Flash) Response:

It is NOT CORRECT that all the mentioned locations lie exactly on the 33rd parallel north. Some are close to the line, but the exact positions vary.

Here is a list of the actual, approximate geographical latitudes of the places you named, north of the Equator:



a) Testing ground for the first atomic bomb detonation (Trinity Site, New Mexico, USA)

Approximate Geographical Latitude 33 Degrees N

Exact Position: Lies south of 33 Degrees N, in the White Sands Missile Range. The Trinity Site is located at approximately 33 Degrees 41' N (or 33.68 Degrees N)



b) Dallas (Texas, USA)

Approximate Geographical Latitude 33 Degrees N

Exact Position: Lies just south of the 33 Degrees N circle. Downtown is located at approximately 32 Degrees 47' N (32.78 Degrees N).



c) Jerusalem (Israel)

Approximate Geographical Latitude 32 Degrees N

Exact Position: Lies significantly south of the 33 Degrees N circle. Approximate position: 31 Degrees 47' N (31.78 Degrees N).



d) Damascus (Syria)

Approximate Geographical Latitude 34 Degrees N

Exact Position: Lies north of the 33 Degrees N circle. Approximate position: 33 Degrees 30' N (33.50 Degrees N).



e) Baghdad (Iraq)

Approximate Geographical Latitude 33 Degrees N

Exact Position: Lies very close to the 33 Degrees N circle, but just north of it. Approximate position: 33 Degrees 20' N (33.33 Degrees N).



f) Confluence of the Euphrates and Tigris (Shatt al-Arab, near Basra, Iraq)

Approximate Geographical Latitude 30 Degrees N

Exact Position: Lies significantly south of the 33 Degrees N circle. The confluence is near 30 Degrees 30' N (30.50 Degrees N).



g) Hiroshima (Japan)

Approximate Geographical Latitude 34 Degrees N

Exact Position: Lies north of the 33 Degrees N circle. Approximate position: 34 Degrees 23' N (34.38 Degrees N).



g) Nagasaki (Japan)

Approximate Geographical Latitude 33 Degrees N

Exact Position: Lies just south of the 33 Degrees N circle. Approximate position: 32 Degrees 45' N (32.75 Degrees N).

Silverstein wasn't actually the sole leaseholder of the World Trade Center, okay? He led a group of investors, okay? We call it a consortium actually, okay? And lenders included GMAC Commercial Mortgage (a General Motors subsidiary), Westfield America Inc. (a shopping center developer), and real estate investor Lloyd Goldman. Silverstein he had obligations to lenders and co-investors, and still owed lease payments of $10 million per month to the Port Authority, okay? So he lost here, a lot. Regarding the moon landing(s). Of course it was a show. Why did the Soviets accept this show? Because they faked all of their space programs. Mind you, German scientists were working in both camps, so they knew each other very well. Fun fact: The Austrian born filmmaker Fritz Lang made a silent movie back in the days in Germany (1927): The Woman in the Moon. It's stunning, how some of the props would be used by NASA, i.e. the countdown and the transfer of the rocket through a movable platform. Fritz Lang said in an interview in 1965:

"Experts from Cape Kennedy asked me a few months ago to come for a talk on rocket ships, and they recognized me as their elder."

Now the Chinese space agency exists and has landed a probe on the other side of the moon, Chang'e 4, is he going to say: this is fake too ? or is it different when China govt. does it ? He will not answer this question) and JFK was not only shot by a second person, and the situation of Oswald's death was suspicious (both reasonable questions to ask), but that it was some kind of Jewish ritual killing. And then he goes on to say, 9/11 was done by more than ~20 people (indeed it was, Saudi Arabia financed it), but that 'fire has never destroyed a skyscraper before or since'? It is true, jet fuel cannot melt steel beams, but it is also true you do not need to melt a thing to seriously impair its structural stability. Material engineering is not this man's specialty and he should not be listened to when he makes claims in this way. And this is before the journey wanders off into nonsense about numerology. The videos about geopolitics are much more interesting.

Overall it's a very interesting channel making claims in an interesting way and I'm not saying that the video shouldn't exist. But to me, charitably, it is a " wrong question, wrong answer ".

### **@cborch555** | 👍 1 likes
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ihh1fdW4-cA&lc=Ugwtyb5yVZgzyUCH5Zh4AaABAg)

The mechanics of the towers collapsing is pretty well supported when you look at the strange method of construction used for the WTC. Normally support columns are distributed throughout the footprint. In the WTC, support columns were concentrated in the center of the building and on the perimeter of the building. AFAIK, this was to allow for wrap-around open office spaces inside while still being strong enough to be tall. 

The outer edge of the building can be thought of as a coke can. You can stand on it without collapsing, but the moment you put a dent in the walls of the can, it crumbles from the overhead weight. Same thing happened with the WTC towers. Heat from the fire after the crash caused weakening of the support linkages holding up the floors above, and when those linkages broke, it was like a whole building slamming down on a dented coke can. 

This is supported by the fact that  the second tower hit was the first to collapse. It was hit lower, so there was more weight above the crash, hence the more rapid failure of the linkages. 


Now, I'm no expert and I could keep providing my cursory understanding of the situation, but I'm purely talking about the engineering of the buildings, the construction of the buildings, and the plausibility of the mechanics of their collapse.
